<article><img alt="Recette Vegan Stew" src="https://www.bulkpowders.fr/the-core-fr/wp-content/uploads/sites/4/2019/10/stew.jpg"/><div><h4>Recette Vegan Stew</h4><p>Retrouvez une nouvelle recette végétarienne! <script>

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
        styleHandler();
    });

    window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
        styleHandler();
    });

    var intervalIds = [];

    function styleHandler () {

        var screenWidth = window.innerWidth,
            scrollPoint = 1100,
            headerWrapper = document.getElementById("headerWrapper"),
            headerElement = document.getElementById("headerElement"),
            headerWrapperChild = document.getElementsByClassName("header--wrapper")[0],
            headerLogoWhole = document.getElementById("headerLogoWhole"),
            headerLogoOnly = document.getElementById("headerLogoOnly"),
            menuPanels = document.querySelectorAll(".mega-menu--panel"),
            basketTrigger = document.getElementById("basketTrigger"),
            basketPanel = document.getElementById("basketPanel"),
            burgerTrigger = document.getElementById("burgerTrigger"),
            burgerPanel = document.getElementById("burgerPanel"),
            closeBurgerPanel = document.getElementById("closeBurgerPanel"),
            closeBasketPanel = document.getElementById("closeBasketPanel"),
            navElement = document.querySelector("nav"),
            bodyElement = document.querySelector("body"),
            megaMenuBehind = document.getElementById("megaMenuBehind"),
            basketPanelBehind = document.getElementById("basketPanelBehind"),
            burgerPanelBehind = document.getElementById("burgerPanelBehind"),
            uspBlocks = document.querySelectorAll(".usp--wrapper .usp--block"), 
            searchInput = document.getElementById("search"),
            newsletter = document.getElementById('newsletter'),
            newsletterSubmit = document.querySelector(".newsletter-form .secondary-button"),
            langPanel = document.getElementById("langPanel"),
            userPanel = document.getElementById("userPanel"),
            pageBreak = 992;

        if (newsletter !== null && newsletter !== undefined) {
            newsletter.addEventListener("input", function () {
                newsletterSubmit.style.display = "inline-flex"
            });
        }

        var bodyScroll = function bodyScroll() {
            if (bodyElement !== null && bodyElement !== undefined) {
                if (bodyElement.getAttribute("data-scroll") === "hidden") {
                    document.querySelector("body").style.overflowY = "scroll";

                    
                    document.querySelector("body").style.position = "relative";
                    bodyElement.setAttribute("data-scroll", "scroll");
                } else {
                    
                    document.querySelector("body").style.overflowY = "hidden";

                    
                    document.querySelector("body").style.position = "fixed";
                    bodyElement.setAttribute("data-scroll", "hidden");
                }
            }
        };

        
        var toggleLang = function toggleLang() {
            if (langPanel.getAttribute("data-visibility") === "hidden") {
                if (screenWidth < pageBreak) {
                    
                    langPanel.setAttribute("data-visibility", "visible");
                    langPanel.style.right = "0";
                    document.getElementById("langPanelBehind").style.display = "block";
                    langPanel.style.width = "calc(100vw - 40px)";
                    bodyScroll();
                } else {
                    
                    langPanel.setAttribute("data-visibility", "visible");
                    langPanel.style.display = "flex";

                    if (window.pageYOffset > 1100) {
                        langPanel.style.top = "74px";
                    } else {
                        langPanel.style.top = "88px";
                    }
                }
            } else {
                
                langPanel.setAttribute("data-visibility", "hidden");

                if (screenWidth <= pageBreak) {
                    
                    langPanel.style.right = "-100vw";
                    document.getElementById("langPanelBehind").style.display = "none";
                    langPanel.style.width = "0";
                    bodyScroll();

                var firstTouch = getTouches(evt)[0];
                xDown = firstTouch.clientX;
                yDown = firstTouch.clientY;
            };

            var handleTouchMove = function handleTouchMove(evt) {
                if (!xDown || !yDown) {
                    return;
                }

                var xUp = evt.touches[0].clientX;
                var yUp = evt.touches[0].clientY;
                var xDiff = xDown - xUp;
                var yDiff = yDown - yUp;

                if (Math.abs(xDiff) > Math.abs(yDiff)) {
                    if (xDiff > 0) {
                        if (burgerPanel.getAttribute("data-visibility") === "visible") {
                            burgerPanel.setAttribute("data-visibility", "hidden");
                            burgerPanel.style.setProperty('left', '-100vw', 'important');

                    } else {
                        
                    }

    function setUspInterval(uspBlocks) {
        var blockCount = 0;
        blockCount = rotateUspBlocks(uspBlocks, blockCount);
        var intervalId = setInterval(function () {
            blockCount = rotateUspBlocks(uspBlocks, blockCount);
        }, 5000);
        return intervalId;
    }

    function resetUspBlocks(uspBlocks) {
        uspBlocks.forEach(function (uspBlock) {
            if (uspBlock !== undefined) {
                if (uspBlock.style.opacity !== undefined) {
                    uspBlock.style.opacity = 1;
                }
            }
        })
    }

    function rotateUspBlocks(uspBlocks, blockCount) {
        if (blockCount === 0) {
            if (uspBlocks[0] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[0].style.opacity = "1";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[1] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[1].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[2] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[2].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            blockCount = 1;
        } else if (blockCount === 1) {
            if (uspBlocks[0] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[0].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[1] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[1].style.opacity = "1";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[2] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[2].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            blockCount = 2;
        } else if (blockCount === 2) {
            if (uspBlocks[0] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[0].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[1] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[1].style.opacity = "0";
            }
            if (uspBlocks[2] !== undefined) {
                uspBlocks[2].style.opacity = "1";
            }
            blockCount = 0;
        };
        return blockCount;
